# KTM 125 SX 2026: aftermarket parts

4697 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 429 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 756 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 3512 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
